becoming a national development issue with
multiplier effects in many parts of society.
President Danny Faure
UNAIDS High-Level event “Fast Track:
Quickening the Pace of Action to end
AIDS” New York, 24 Sept. 2017
“We are all involved”
Dr Patrick Herminie, 4 June 2018
Secretary of State For the Prevention of Drug
Abuse and Rehabilitation
